- **find-ux-gaps**: Finds usability gaps and pre-release rough edges in an existing app (from source, plus the browser when it runs) and turns them into evidence-backed tickets, written to markdown and filed to GitHub, GitLab, Jira or Azure DevOps only after a yes. Use for "find UX gaps", "file UX tickets", "what should we polish before launch". Not for a screenshot-backed QA/UX report without tickets (use audit-app) or a review of one branch's diff (use review-branch). ([parvez-ahammed/shipkit](https://github.com/parvez-ahammed/shipkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/shipkit/skills/find-ux-gaps))

- **find-verbs**: Interactively populate or update named verb lists (`setup`, `check`, or custom) in a project's top-level `sdlc.yaml`. For `check` and `setup`, probes the project root via `sdlc quality detect` and `sdlc project detect-setup` respectively, presents the detected candidates through AskUserQuestion (multiSelect), and writes the approved subset back to sdlc.yaml. For other names, edits the current list without probing. Idempotent — re-running with the same selection is a no-op. Runs standalone or as a called step of `/sdlc:setup`. ([osdlc/osdlc](https://github.com/osdlc/osdlc/tree/HEAD/.claude-plugin/packs/sdlc/skills/find-verbs) · ★3 · 3 copies)
